Appendix A
Equations of Motions and
\mathcal{I}
-tensors

The EoM for the field theory is substantially complicated due to the graviton sector. This makes the derivation of the DS equation difficult. As we have discussed in Section
2.1
, it is efficient to introduce the
\mathcal{I}
-tensors. Recall that the perturbed Einstein equation in (
22
) is decomposed into two parts,
\mathcal{G}^{\mu\nu}
and
\mathcal{T}^{\mu\nu}
. The general form of the curvature perturbations
\mathcal{G}^{\mu\nu}_{n}
is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{\mu\nu}_{n}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,M_{1},\cdots,M_{n-1},\kappa N,\lambda P}_{n,1}h^{M_{1}}\cdots h^{M_{n-1}}\partial_{\kappa}h^{N}\partial_{\lambda}h^{P}

(227)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,M_{1},\cdots,M_{n-1},N,\kappa\lambda P}_{n,2}h^{M_{1}}\cdots h^{M_{n-1}}h^{N}\partial_{\kappa}\partial_{\lambda}h^{P}\,.

The general form of the
n
-th order of the energy-momentum perturbations
\mathcal{T}^{\mu\nu}_{n}
are given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\mu\nu}_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{I=1}^{2}\left[\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{\mu\nu,N_{1},\cdots N_{n-1},I}h^{N_{1}}\cdots h^{N_{n-1}}\varphi_{I}^{2}\right]\,,\qquad n>1\,.

(228)

The EoM of the scalar fields are defined in (
25
). The general
n
-th order term (for
n>1
) of the EoM is also denoted by

\mathcal{I}^{I,M_{1},M_{2},\cdots,M_{n}}_{\phi}h^{M_{1}}h^{M_{2}}\cdots h^{M_{n}}\phi^{I}\,.

(229)

Here we denote the
\mathcal{I}
-tensors explicitly associated with the classical two-loop amplitudes. The relevant
\mathcal{I}
-tensors are as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1,1}^{\mu\nu,\rho_{1}\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1,2}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\,,

(230)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,1}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,2}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},I}_{\rm EM}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2},\lambda_{2},I}_{\rm EM}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{I,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}_{\phi}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{I,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}_{\phi}\,.

A.1
Graviton sector

Let us consider the curvature tensor part,
\mathcal{G}^{\mu\nu}_{1}
and
\mathcal{G}^{\mu\nu}_{2}

using the
\mathcal{I}
-tensors. First
\mathcal{G}^{\mu\nu}_{1}
is defined in (
23
) as

\mathcal{G}_{1}^{\mu\nu}=\mathcal{I}_{1,1}^{\mu\nu,\rho_{1}\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\partial_{\rho_{1}}h_{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\partial_{\rho_{2}}h_{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}+\mathcal{I}_{1,2}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}h_{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\partial_{\rho_{1}}\partial_{\rho_{2}}h_{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\,.

(231)

We want to present the explicit form of the
\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\rho_{1}\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}_{1,1}
and
\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}_{1,2}
. For simplicity, we decompose the
\mathcal{I}
-tensors according to the following tensor structure:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1,1}^{\mu\nu,\rho_{1}\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{1,1}^{\mu\nu,\rho_{1}\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}+\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{1,1}^{\mu\nu,\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{1}\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\,,

(232)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1,2}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{1,2}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}+\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{1,2}^{\nu\mu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\,,

where

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{1,1}}^{\mu\nu,\rho_{1}\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}

(233)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\mu\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\eta^{\kappa_{1}(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\lambda_{1}}+\frac{1}{16}\eta^{\rho_{1}(\mu}\eta^{\nu)\rho_{2}}\eta^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\mu\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})\rho_{2}}\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\rho_{2}(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})(\mu}\eta^{\nu)(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\rho_{1}}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\rho_{1}(\mu}\eta^{\nu)\rho_{2}}\eta^{\kappa_{1}(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\lambda_{1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})\rho_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\rho_{1}(\mu}\eta^{\nu)({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\rho_{2}}\,,

and

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{1,2}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}

(234)

\displaystyle=\eta^{\mu(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})\nu}\bigg[\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\rho_{2}}\bigg]-\frac{\eta^{\mu\nu}}{8}\bigg[\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\eta^{\kappa_{1}(\rho_{1}}\eta^{\rho_{2})\lambda_{1}}-\eta^{\kappa_{1}(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\lambda_{2}}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\lambda_{2}}}\eta^{{\kappa_{2}})(\rho_{1}}\eta^{\rho_{2})\nu}\,.

Similarly, the
\mathcal{G}^{\mu\nu}_{2}
is defined in (
23
) as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{\mu\nu}_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,1}h^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\partial_{\rho_{1}}h^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\partial_{\rho_{2}}h^{\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}

(235)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,2}h^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}h^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\partial_{\rho_{1}}\partial_{\rho_{2}}h^{\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}\,.

Again, we decompose the
\mathcal{I}
-tensors as the

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,1}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{I}}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,1}+\bar{\mathcal{I}}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3},\rho_{1}\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}_{2,1}

(236)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,2}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{I}}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,2}+\bar{\mathcal{I}}^{\nu\mu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{2,2}\,,

where

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{2,1}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\rho_{1}\lambda_{2}\kappa_{2},\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}

(237)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{\mu\nu}}{8}\Big[2\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}\eta^{{\kappa_{1}}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{1}}}-\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\rho_{2}}\eta^{{\kappa_{2}}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{2}}}-\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\rho_{2}}\eta^{{\kappa_{1}}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{1}}}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\rho_{1}(\mu}\eta^{\nu)\rho_{2}}\Big[2\eta^{{\kappa_{1}}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{1}}}-\eta^{{\kappa_{1}}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}}){\lambda_{1}}}\eta^{{\kappa_{3}}{\lambda_{3}}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\nu}\Big[\eta^{{\kappa_{2}}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{2}}}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}+\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})\rho_{2}}\eta^{{\kappa_{2}}{\lambda_{2}}}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}}{4}\Big[\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})\nu}+\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\nu}+\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\Big[\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\nu}+\eta^{\nu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\mu}\Big]\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})\rho_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\rho_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\rho_{1}(\mu}\eta^{\nu)({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\rho_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\rho_{1}(\mu}\eta^{\nu)({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\rho_{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})(\mu}\eta^{\nu)({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1})}{(\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\rho_{2}}\,,

and

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{2,2}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\rho_{1}\rho_{2}\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}

(238)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{\mu\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}}{16}\Big[\eta^{{\kappa_{1}}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2})}{(\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{1}}}+\eta^{\lambda_{1}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2})}{(\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){{\kappa_{1}}}}\Big]+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\nu}\eta^{{\kappa_{3}}{\lambda_{3}}}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta^{\mu\nu}}{16}\Big[\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\rho_{2}}\eta^{{\kappa_{1}}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{1}}}+\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\rho_{2}}\eta^{{\kappa_{2}}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{2}}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\nu}}{16}\Big[\eta^{{\kappa_{2}}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{2}}}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}-\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\rho_{2}}\eta^{{\kappa_{3}}{\lambda_{3}}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\nu}}{16}\Big[\eta^{{\kappa_{1}}({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}}){\lambda_{1}}}\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}+\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\rho_{2}}\eta^{{\kappa_{3}}{\lambda_{3}}}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta^{\rho_{1}\rho_{2}}}{4}\Big[\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})\nu}+\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\nu}+\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\nu}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})\rho_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})\nu}\eta^{\rho_{1}({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\rho_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})(\rho_{1}}\eta^{\rho_{2})\nu}+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\mu(\rho_{1}}\eta^{\rho_{2})({\kappa_{3}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{3}})({\kappa_{2}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{2}})({\kappa_{1}}}\eta^{{\lambda_{1}})\nu}\,.

Let us consider the energy-momentum tensor part. Since the
\mathcal{T}^{M}_{1}
does not involve the
\mathcal{I}
-tensor, we focus on the
\mathcal{T}^{M}_{2}
and
\mathcal{T}^{M}_{3}
,

\mathcal{T}^{M}_{2}=\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{M,N}\Theta^{N}_{2}\,,\qquad\mathcal{T}^{M}_{3}=\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{M,N,P}\Theta^{N,P}_{3}\,,

(239)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}_{\rm EM}=\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})\nu}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu\nu}\eta^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\,,

(240)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\mu\nu,\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}_{\rm EM}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16}\eta^{\mu\nu}\eta^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\mu\nu}\eta^{\kappa_{2}(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})\lambda_{2}}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\mu(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})\nu}\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}

(241)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\mu(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\nu}\eta^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\nu}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\nu(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\mu}\,.

Finally,
\mathcal{I}
-tensors that appear in the EoM of
\phi_{I}
(
25
) are

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}_{\phi}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\kappa_{1}(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\lambda_{1}}\,,

(242)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1},\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2},\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}_{\phi}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{48}\eta^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\eta^{\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\kappa_{1}\lambda_{1}}\eta^{\kappa_{2}(\kappa_{3}}\eta^{\lambda_{3})\lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\kappa_{2}\lambda_{2}}\eta^{\kappa_{3}(\kappa_{1}}\eta^{\lambda_{1})\lambda_{3}}+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{\kappa_{3}\lambda_{3}}\eta^{\kappa_{1}(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})\lambda_{1}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{12}\eta^{\kappa_{1}(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})(\kappa_{3}}\eta^{\lambda_{3})\lambda_{1}}-\frac{1}{12}\eta^{\lambda_{1}(\kappa_{2}}\eta^{\lambda_{2})(\kappa_{3}}\eta^{\lambda_{3})\kappa_{1}}\,.

Appendix B
Descendant equations

B.1
Deformation rule for the product of multiple fields

We explain the general structure of the deformation, which is a key step in deriving the DS equation. We first consider arbitrary fields
A_{x},B_{x},C_{x},D_{x}
and their external sources
a_{x},b_{x},c_{x},d_{x}
respectively. The deformation is defined as follows:

\displaystyle A_{x}\mapsto A_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\frac{\delta}{\delta a_{x}}\,,\qquad B_{x}\mapsto B_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\frac{\delta}{\delta b_{x}}\,,

(243)

\displaystyle C_{x}\mapsto C_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\frac{\delta}{\delta c_{x}}\,,\qquad D_{x}\mapsto D_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\frac{\delta}{\delta d_{x}}\,.

We introduce a shorthand notation for the functional derivatives as

\displaystyle\psi^{ab}_{xy}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta A_{x}}{\delta b_{y}}\,,

\displaystyle\quad\psi^{bc}_{yz}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta B_{y}}{\delta c_{z}}\,,

\displaystyle\quad\psi^{ca}_{zx}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta C_{z}}{\delta a_{x}}\,,

(244)

\displaystyle\psi^{abc}_{xyz}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta^{2}A_{x}}{\delta b_{y}\delta c_{z}}\,,

\displaystyle\quad\psi^{bca}_{yzx}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta^{2}B_{y}}{\delta c_{z}\delta a_{x}}\,,

\displaystyle\quad\psi^{cab}_{zxy}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta^{2}C_{z}}{\delta a_{x}\delta b_{y}}\,,

and their permutations as

\displaystyle\hat{\psi}^{ab}_{xy}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\Big(\psi^{ab}_{xy}+\psi^{ba}_{yx}\Big)\,,

(245)

\displaystyle\hat{\psi}^{abc}_{xyz}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3!}\Big(\psi_{xyz}^{abc}+\psi_{xzy}^{acb}+\psi_{yxz}^{bac}+\psi_{yzx}^{bca}+\psi_{zxy}^{cab}+\psi_{zyx}^{cba}\Big)\,.

Under the deformation, we derive the following rules:

\displaystyle A_{x}B_{y}\mapsto A_{x}B_{y}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\hat{\psi}^{ab}_{x,y}\,,

(246)

\displaystyle A_{x}B_{y}C_{z}\mapsto A_{x}B_{y}C_{z}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\left[A_{x}\hat{\psi}_{yz}^{bc}+B_{y}\hat{\psi}_{zx}^{ca}+C_{z}\hat{\psi}_{xy}^{ab}\right]+\Big(\frac{\hbar}{i}\Big)^{2}\hat{\psi}_{xyz}^{abc}\,,

\displaystyle A_{x}B_{y}C_{z}D_{w}\mapsto A_{x}B_{y}C_{z}D_{w}

\displaystyle+\frac{\hbar}{i}\bigg[\hat{\psi}^{ab}_{xy}C_{z}D_{w}+\hat{\psi}^{ac}_{xz}B_{y}D_{w}+\hat{\psi}^{ad}_{xw}B_{y}C_{z}+\hat{\psi}^{bc}_{yz}A_{x}D_{w}+\hat{\psi}^{bd}_{yw}A_{x}C_{z}+\hat{\psi}^{cd}_{zw}A_{x}B_{y}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{\hbar}{i}\Big)^{2}\bigg[A_{x}\hat{\psi}_{yzw}^{bcd}+B_{y}\hat{\psi}_{xzw}^{acd}+C_{z}\hat{\psi}_{xyw}^{abd}+D_{w}\hat{\psi}_{xyz}^{abc}+\hat{\psi}^{ab}_{xy}\hat{\psi}^{cd}_{zw}+\hat{\psi}^{ac}_{xz}\hat{\psi}^{bd}_{yw}+\hat{\psi}^{ad}_{xw}\hat{\psi}^{bc}_{yz}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{\hbar}{i}\Big)^{3}\hat{\psi}_{xyzw}^{abcd}\,.

B.2
Explicit form of the DS equations

The DS equation for
\varphi^{I}_{x}
is given by

\displaystyle\left(\square-m_{I}^{2}\right)\varphi^{I}_{x}=-\rho_{x}^{I}+\kappa\Omega^{I}_{1}+\kappa^{2}\mathcal{I}^{I,M,N}_{\phi}\Omega^{IMN}_{2}+\kappa^{3}\mathcal{I}^{I,M,N,P}_{\phi}\Omega^{IMNP}_{3}\,,

(247)

where

\displaystyle\Omega^{I}_{1}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\kappa}\big(h_{x}^{\kappa\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}\varphi_{x}^{I}\big)-\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}h^{\kappa\kappa}_{x}\varphi_{x}^{I}+\left(\partial_{\kappa}^{y}\partial_{\lambda}^{x}+\partial_{\kappa}^{x}\partial_{\lambda}^{x}\right)\hat{\psi}_{xy}^{I\kappa\lambda}\Big|_{y\to x}-\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\hat{\psi}^{\kappa\kappa,I}_{xx}\,,

(248)

\displaystyle\Omega^{IMN}_{2}

\displaystyle=h_{x}^{M}h_{x}^{N}\varphi_{x}^{I}+\hat{\psi}_{xx}^{MN}\varphi^{I}_{x}+2h^{(M}_{x}\hat{\psi}_{xx}^{N)I}+\hat{\psi}_{xxx}^{MNI}\,,

\displaystyle\Omega^{IMNP}_{3}

\displaystyle=h_{x}^{M}h_{x}^{N}h_{x}^{P}\varphi_{x}^{I}+3\Big[h^{(M}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{NP)}_{xx}\varphi^{I}_{x}+h^{(M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{P)I}_{x,x}\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big[\hat{\psi}^{MNP}_{xxx}\varphi^{I}_{x}+3h^{(M}\hat{\psi}_{xxx}^{NP)I}+3\hat{\psi}^{(MN}_{xx}\hat{\psi}^{P)I}_{xx}\Big]\,,

where

\displaystyle\hat{\psi}^{AB}_{xy}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\hbar}{i}\left(\psi^{AB}_{xy}+\psi^{BA}_{yx}\right)\,,

(249)

\displaystyle\hat{\psi}_{xyz}^{ABC}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3!}\bigg(\frac{\hbar}{i}\bigg)^{2}\left(\psi_{xyz}^{ABC}+\psi_{xzy}^{ACB}+\psi_{yzx}^{BCA}+\psi_{yxz}^{BAC}+\psi_{zxy}^{CAB}+\psi_{zyx}^{CBA}\right)\,.

The DS equation for
h^{M}
is given by

\Box h_{x}^{M}=2P^{M,N}\bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{2}\kappa^{n}\mathcal{G}^{N}_{n}-\sum_{n=1}^{3}\kappa^{n}\mathcal{T}^{N}_{n}-2j^{N}_{x}\bigg)\,,

(250)

where
\mathcal{G}^{M}_{x}
represents the curvature perturbations

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{M}_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}^{M,\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}\Lambda^{\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}+\mathcal{I}^{M,N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}\Lambda^{N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}\,,

(251)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{M}_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}^{M,N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}\Lambda^{N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}+\mathcal{I}^{M,N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}\Lambda^{N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}\,,

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{M}_{1}=\Theta^{M}_{1}\,,\qquad\mathcal{T}^{M}_{2}=\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{M,N}\Theta^{N}_{2}\,,\qquad\mathcal{T}^{M}_{3}=\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{M,N,P}\Theta^{N,P}_{3}\,.

(252)

The explicit structure of
\Lambda
and
\Theta
are given by

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\rho}h^{N}_{x}\partial_{\sigma}h^{P}_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\partial^{x}_{\rho}\partial^{y}_{\sigma}\hat{\psi}_{xy}^{NP}\Big|_{y\to x}\,,

(253)

\displaystyle\Lambda^{N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}

\displaystyle=h^{N}_{x}\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\sigma}h^{P}_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\partial^{y}_{\rho}\partial^{y}_{\sigma}\hat{\psi}_{xy}^{NP}\Big|_{y\to x}\,,

\displaystyle\Lambda^{N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}

\displaystyle=h^{N}_{x}\partial_{\rho}h^{P}_{x}\partial_{\sigma}h^{Q}_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\Big[\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{y}\hat{\psi}_{xy}^{PQ}h^{N}_{x}+\partial_{\rho}^{y}\hat{\psi}^{NP}_{xy}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}h_{x}^{Q}+\partial_{\sigma}^{y}\hat{\psi}^{NQ}_{xy}\partial^{x}_{\rho}h_{x}^{P}\Big]_{y\to x}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\hbar}{i}\right)^{2}\partial_{\rho}^{y}\partial_{\sigma}^{z}\hat{\psi}_{xyz}^{NPQ}\Big|_{y,z\to x}

\displaystyle\Lambda^{N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}

\displaystyle=h^{N}_{x}h^{P}_{x}\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\sigma}h^{Q}_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\Big[\hat{\psi}_{xy}^{NP}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}h_{x}^{Q}+h^{N}_{x}\partial_{\rho}^{y}\partial_{\sigma}^{y}\hat{\psi}_{xy}^{PQ}+h^{P}_{x}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\hat{\psi}_{xy}^{QN}\Big]_{y\to x}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\hbar}{i}\right)^{2}\partial_{\rho}^{z}\partial_{\sigma}^{z}\hat{\psi}_{xyz}^{NPQ}\Big|_{y,z\to x}

where

\displaystyle\Theta^{\mu\nu}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{I=1}^{2}\Bigg[\partial_{\mu}\varphi^{I}_{x}\partial_{\nu}\varphi^{I}_{x}+\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\eta_{\mu\nu}\varphi^{I}_{x}\varphi^{I}_{x}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\bigg(\partial^{x}_{\mu}\partial^{y}_{\nu}+\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\eta_{\mu\nu}\bigg)\hat{\psi}^{I,I}_{x,y}\Big|_{y\to x}\Bigg]\,,

(254)

\displaystyle\Theta^{N}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{I=1}^{2}m^{2}_{I}\left[h^{N}_{x}(\varphi^{I}_{x})^{2}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\Big(2\hat{\psi}^{N,I}_{x,x}\varphi^{I}_{x}+h^{N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{I,I}_{x,x}\Big)+\left(\frac{\hbar}{i}\right)^{2}\hat{\psi}^{N,I,I}_{x,x,x}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\Theta^{N,P}_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{I=1}^{2}m^{2}_{I}\left[h^{N}_{x}h^{P}_{x}(\varphi^{I}_{x})^{2}+\frac{\hbar}{i}\Big(\hat{\psi}_{x,x}^{N,P}(\varphi^{I}_{x})^{2}+h^{(N}_{x}h^{P)}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{I,I}_{x,x}+4h^{(N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{P),I}_{x,x}\varphi^{I}_{x}\Big)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\frac{\hbar}{i}\right)^{2}\Big(3\hat{\psi}^{(N,P}_{x,x}\hat{\psi}^{I),I}_{x,x}+2\hat{\psi}^{N,P,I}_{x,x,x}\varphi^{I}_{x}+2h^{(N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{P),I,I}_{x,x,x}\Big)\right]\,.

B.3
DS equations for the descendant fields

To solve the DS equations, we need to derive the equations for the descendant fields. It is straightforward to achieve by acting the functional derivative
\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathbf{j}^{A}_{x}}
on the DS equations for
h^{\mu\nu}
and
\varphi^{I}
.

Let us consider the scalar field sector. The DS equation for the first descendant fields
\psi^{I,A}
are given by

\displaystyle\left(\square-m_{I}^{2}\right)\psi^{IA}_{xy}

\displaystyle=-\hat{\delta}_{xy}\delta^{IA}+\kappa\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Omega^{I}_{1}\big]+\kappa^{2}\mathcal{I}^{M,N}_{\varphi,I}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Omega^{IMN}_{2}\big]+\kappa^{3}\mathcal{I}^{M,N,P}_{\varphi,I}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Omega^{IMNP}_{3}\big]\,,

(255)

where

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Omega^{I}_{1}\big]

\displaystyle=\partial^{x}_{\kappa}\Big(\psi^{\kappa\lambda A}_{xy}\partial_{\lambda}\varphi_{x}^{I}+h^{\kappa\lambda}_{x}\partial^{x}_{\lambda}\psi^{IA}_{xy}\Big)-\frac{m^{2}_{I}}{2}\Big(\psi^{\lambda\lambda A}_{xy}\varphi^{I}_{x}+h_{x}^{\lambda\lambda}\psi^{IA}_{xy}\Big)

(256)

\displaystyle+\bigg[\left(\partial_{\kappa}^{x}\partial_{\lambda}^{x}+\partial_{\kappa}^{z}\partial_{\lambda}^{x}\right)\hat{\psi}_{xz|y}^{I\kappa\lambda|A}-\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\hat{\psi}^{\lambda\lambda I|A}_{xz|y}\bigg]_{z\to x}\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Omega^{IMN}_{2}\big]

\displaystyle=2h^{(M}_{x}\psi^{N)A}_{xy}\varphi^{I}_{x}+h^{M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}\psi^{I,A}_{x,y}

\displaystyle+\Big[\hat{\psi}^{MN|A}_{xz|y}\varphi^{I}_{x}+\hat{\psi}^{MN}_{xz}\psi^{IA}_{xy}+2\psi^{(M|A|}_{xy}\hat{\psi}^{N)I}_{xz}+2h^{(M}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{N)I|A}_{xz|y}\Big]_{z\to x}\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Omega^{IMNP}_{3}\big]

\displaystyle=3h^{(M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}\psi^{P)A}_{xy}\varphi^{I}_{x}+h^{M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}h^{P}_{x}\psi^{IA}_{xy}

\displaystyle+3\Big[\psi^{(M|A|}_{xy}\hat{\psi}^{NP)}_{xz}\varphi^{I}_{x}+h^{(M}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{NP)|A}_{xz|y}\varphi^{I}_{x}+h^{(M}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{NP)}_{xz}\psi^{IA}_{xy}

\displaystyle+2\psi^{(M|A|}_{xy}h^{N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{P)I}_{xz}+h^{(M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{P)I|A}_{xz|y}\Big]_{z\to x}\,,

Here we denote the functional derivative to the first descendant fields as

\displaystyle\hat{\psi}^{AB|C}_{xy|z}:=\frac{\delta\hat{\psi}^{AB}_{xy}}{\delta\mathbf{j}^{C}_{z}}=\frac{1}{2}\Big(\psi_{xyz}^{ABC}+\psi_{yxz}^{BAC}\Big)\,.

(257)

Next, the DS equation for second descendant fields
\psi^{I,AB}_{x,y,z}

\displaystyle\left(\square-m_{I}^{2}\right)\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}=\kappa\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Omega^{I}_{1}\big]+\kappa^{2}\mathcal{I}^{M,N}_{\varphi,I}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Omega^{IMN}_{2}\big]+\kappa^{3}\mathcal{I}^{M,N,P}_{\varphi,I}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Omega^{IMNP}_{3}\big]\,,

(258)

where

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Omega^{I}_{1}\big]

\displaystyle=\partial_{\kappa}\left(\psi^{\kappa\lambda AB}_{xyz}\partial_{\lambda}\varphi^{I}_{x}+\psi_{xy}^{\kappa\lambda A}\partial_{\lambda}\psi^{IB}_{xz}+\psi_{xz}^{\kappa\lambda B}\partial_{\lambda}\psi^{IA}_{xy}+h_{x}^{\kappa\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}\right)

(259)

\displaystyle-\frac{m^{2}_{I}}{2}\Big(\psi^{\lambda\lambda AB}_{xyz}\varphi^{I}_{x}+\psi^{\lambda\lambda A}_{xy}\psi^{IB}_{xz}+\psi_{xz}^{\lambda\lambda B}\psi^{IA}_{xy}+h_{x}^{\lambda\lambda}\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}\Big)\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Omega^{IMN}_{2}\big]

\displaystyle=2\Big(\psi^{(M|B|}_{x,z}\psi^{N)A}_{xy}+h^{(M}_{x}\psi^{N)AB}_{xyz}\Big)\varphi^{I}_{x}+2h^{(M}_{x}\psi^{N)A}_{xy}\psi^{IB}_{xz}

\displaystyle+2h^{(M}_{x}\psi^{N)B}_{xz}\psi^{IA}_{xy}+h^{M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Omega^{IMNP}_{3}\big]

\displaystyle=6h^{(M}_{x}\psi^{N|B|}_{xz}\psi^{P)A}_{xy}\varphi^{I}_{x}+3h^{(M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}\psi^{P)AB}_{xyz}\varphi^{I}_{x}

\displaystyle+3h^{(M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}\psi^{P)A}_{xy}\psi^{IB}_{xz}+3h^{(M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}\psi^{P)B}_{xz}\psi^{IA}_{xy}+h^{M}_{x}h^{N}_{x}h^{P}_{x}\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}\,.

We then consider the graviton sector. The first descendent equation for the EoM of
h^{\mu\nu}
is given by a functional derivative with respect to
\mathbf{j}^{A}
to the EoM

\Box\psi_{xy}^{MA}=2P^{M,N}\Big(\sum_{n=1}^{2}\kappa^{n}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\mathcal{G}^{N}_{n}\big]-\sum_{n=1}^{3}\kappa^{n}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[{\mathcal{T}}^{N}_{n}\big]-2\hat{\delta}_{x,y}\delta^{N,A}\Big)\,,

(260)

where the
\Delta
operation on
\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\mathcal{G}^{M}_{1}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}^{M,\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Lambda^{\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}\big]+\mathcal{I}^{M,N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Lambda^{N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}\big]\,,

(261)

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\mathcal{G}^{M}_{2}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}^{M,N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Lambda^{N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}\big]+\mathcal{I}^{M,N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Lambda^{N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}\big]\,,

and
\Delta
operation on
\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\mathcal{T}^{M}_{1}\big]=\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Theta^{M}_{1}\big]\,,

(262)

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\mathcal{T}^{M}_{2}\big]=\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{M,N}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Theta^{N}_{2}\big]\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\mathcal{T}^{M}_{3}\big]=\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{M,N,P}\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Theta^{N,P}_{3}\big]\,.

with

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Lambda^{\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}\big]

\displaystyle=\partial^{x}_{\rho}\psi^{NA}_{xy}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}h^{P}_{xy}+\partial^{x}_{\rho}h^{N}_{x}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}\psi^{PA}_{xy}+\partial^{x}_{\rho}\partial^{z}_{\sigma}\hat{\psi}_{xz|y}^{NP|A}\Big|_{z\to x}\,,

(263)

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Lambda^{N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}\big]

\displaystyle=\psi^{NA}_{xy}\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\sigma}h^{P}_{x}+h^{N}_{x}\partial^{x}_{\rho}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}\psi^{PA}_{xy}+\partial^{z}_{\rho}\partial^{z}_{\sigma}\hat{\psi}_{xz|y}^{NP|A}\Big|_{z\to x}\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Lambda^{N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}\big]

\displaystyle=\psi^{NA}_{xy}\partial_{\rho}h^{P}_{x}\partial_{\sigma}h^{Q}_{x}+h^{N}_{x}\partial^{x}_{\rho}\psi^{PA}_{xy}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}h^{Q}_{x}+h^{N}_{x}\partial^{x}_{\rho}h^{P}_{x}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}\psi^{QA}_{xy}

\displaystyle+\Big[\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{z}\hat{\psi}_{xz|y}^{PQ|A}h^{N}_{x}+\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{z}\hat{\psi}_{xz}^{PQ}\psi^{NA}_{xy}+\partial_{\rho}^{y}\hat{\psi}^{NP|A}_{xz|y}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}h_{x}^{Q}

\displaystyle+\partial_{\rho}^{y}\hat{\psi}^{NP}_{xz}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}\psi_{xy}^{QA}+\partial_{\sigma}^{y}\hat{\psi}^{NQ|A}_{xz|y}\partial^{x}_{\rho}h_{x}^{P}+\partial_{\sigma}^{y}\hat{\psi}^{NQ}_{xy}\partial^{x}_{\rho}\psi_{xy}^{PA}\Big]_{z\to x}\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Lambda^{N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}\big]

\displaystyle=\psi^{NA}_{xy}h^{P}_{x}\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\sigma}h^{Q}_{x}+h^{N}_{x}\psi^{PA}_{xy}\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\sigma}h^{Q}_{x}+h^{N}_{x}h^{P}_{x}\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\sigma}\psi^{QA}_{xy}

\displaystyle+\Big[\hat{\psi}_{xzy}^{NPA}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}h_{x}^{Q}+\hat{\psi}_{xz}^{NP}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi_{xy}^{QA}+\psi^{NA}_{xy}\partial_{\rho}^{z}\partial_{\sigma}^{z}\hat{\psi}_{xz}^{PQ}

\displaystyle+h^{N}_{x}\partial_{\rho}^{z}\partial_{\sigma}^{z}\hat{\psi}_{xz|y}^{PQ|A}+\psi^{PA}_{xy}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\hat{\psi}_{xz}^{QN}+h^{P}_{x}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\hat{\psi}_{xz|y}^{QN|A}\Big]_{z\to x}\,,

and

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Theta^{\kappa\lambda}_{1}\big]

\displaystyle=\partial^{x}_{\kappa}\psi^{IA}_{xy}\partial^{x}_{\lambda}\varphi^{I}_{x}+\partial^{x}_{\kappa}\varphi^{I}_{x}\partial^{x}_{\lambda}\psi^{IA}_{xy}+m_{I}^{2}\eta_{\kappa\lambda}\psi^{IA}_{xy}\varphi^{I}_{x}+\Big(\partial^{x}_{\kappa}\partial^{y}_{\lambda}+\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\eta_{\kappa\lambda}\Big)\hat{\psi}^{II|A}_{xz|y}\Big|_{z\to x}\,,

(264)

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Theta^{N,I}_{2}\big]

\displaystyle=\psi^{NA}_{xy}(\varphi^{I}_{x})^{2}+2h^{N}_{x}\varphi^{I}_{x}\psi^{IA}_{xy}

\displaystyle+\Big[2\hat{\psi}^{NI|A}_{xx|y}\varphi^{I}_{x}+2\hat{\psi}^{NI}_{xx}\varphi^{IA}_{xy}+\psi^{NA}_{xy}\hat{\psi}^{II}_{xx}+h^{N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{II|A}_{xx|y}\Big]\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A}_{y}\big[\Theta^{N,P,I}_{3}\big]

\displaystyle=2\psi^{(N|A|}_{xy}h^{P)}_{x}(\varphi^{I}_{x})^{2}+2h^{N}_{x}h^{P}_{x}\varphi^{I}_{x}\psi^{IA}_{xy}

\displaystyle+\Big[\hat{\psi}_{xx|y}^{NP|A}(\varphi^{I}_{x})^{2}+2\hat{\psi}_{xx}^{N,P}\varphi^{I}_{x}\psi^{IA}_{xy}+2\psi^{(N|A|}_{xy}h^{P)}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{II}_{xx}+h^{N}_{x}h^{P}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{II|A}_{xx|y}

\displaystyle+4\psi^{(N|A|}_{xy}\hat{\psi}^{P)I}_{xx}\varphi^{I}_{x}+4h^{(N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{P)I|A}_{xx|y}\varphi^{I}_{x}+4h^{(N}_{x}\hat{\psi}^{P)I}_{xx}\psi^{IA}_{x}\,.\Big]

Next, the DS equation for the second descendent field up to
\hbar^{2}
order is given by

\Box\psi_{xy}^{MAB}=2P^{M,N}\Big(\sum_{n=1}^{2}\kappa^{n}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\mathcal{G}^{N}_{n}\big]-\sum_{n=1}^{3}\kappa^{n}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[{\mathcal{T}}^{N}_{n}\big]\Big)\,,

(265)

where the
\Delta
operations on
\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\mathcal{G}^{M}_{1}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}^{M,\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Lambda^{\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}\big]+\mathcal{I}^{M,N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Lambda^{N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}\big]\,,

(266)

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\mathcal{G}^{M}_{2}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}^{M,N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Lambda^{N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}\big]+\mathcal{I}^{M,N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Lambda^{N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}\big]\,,

and
\Delta
operation on
\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\mathcal{T}^{M}_{1}\big]=\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Theta^{M}_{1}\big]\,,

(267)

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\mathcal{T}^{M}_{2}\big]=\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{M,N}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Theta^{N}_{2}\big]\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\mathcal{T}^{M}_{3}\big]=\mathcal{I}_{\rm EM}^{M,N,P}\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Theta^{N,P}_{3}\big]\,.

with

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Lambda^{\rho N,\sigma P}_{1,1}\big]

\displaystyle=\partial_{\rho}^{x}\psi_{xyz}^{NAB}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}h_{x}^{P}+2\partial_{\rho}^{x}\psi^{N(A}_{xy}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi_{xz}^{|P|B)}+\partial_{\rho}^{x}h_{x}^{N}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi_{xyz}^{PAB}\,,

(268)

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Lambda^{N,\rho\sigma P}_{1,2}\big]

\displaystyle=\psi^{NAB}_{xyz}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}h_{x}^{P}+\psi^{NA}_{xy}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi_{xz}^{PB}+\psi^{NB}_{xz}\partial^{x}_{\rho}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}\psi^{PA}_{xy}+h_{x}^{N}\partial^{x}_{\rho}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}\psi^{NAB}_{xyz}\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Lambda^{N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{2,1}\big]

\displaystyle=\psi^{NAB}_{xyz}\partial_{\rho}^{x}h_{x}^{P}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}h_{x}^{Q}+h_{x}^{N}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\psi^{PAB}_{xyz}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}h_{x}^{Q}+h_{x}^{N}\partial_{\rho}^{x}h_{x}^{P}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi^{QAB}_{xyz}

\displaystyle+\psi^{NA}_{xy}(\partial_{\rho}^{x}\psi_{xz}^{PB}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}h_{x}^{Q}+\partial_{\rho}^{x}h_{x}^{P}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi^{QB}_{xz})+\psi^{NB}_{xz}(\partial_{\rho}^{x}\psi^{PA}_{xy}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}h_{x}^{Q}+\partial_{\rho}^{x}h_{x}^{P}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi^{QA}_{xy})

\displaystyle+h_{x}^{N}(\partial_{\rho}^{x}\psi^{PA}_{xy}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}\psi^{QB}_{xz}+\partial^{x}_{\rho}\psi^{PB}_{xz}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi^{QA}_{xy})\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Lambda^{N,P,\rho\sigma Q}_{2,2}\big]

\displaystyle=2h_{x}^{(N}\psi^{P)AB}_{xyz}\partial^{x}_{\rho}\partial^{x}_{\sigma}h_{x}^{Q}+h_{x}^{N}h_{x}^{P}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi_{xyz}^{QAB}+(\psi^{NA}_{xy}\psi^{PB}_{xz}+\psi^{NB}_{xz}\psi^{PA}_{xy})\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}h_{x}^{Q}

\displaystyle+2h_{x}^{(N}\psi^{P)A}_{xy}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi^{QB}_{xz}+2h_{x}^{(N}\psi^{P)B}_{xz}\partial_{\rho}^{x}\partial_{\sigma}^{x}\psi^{QA}_{xy}\,,

and

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Theta^{\kappa\lambda}_{1}\big]

\displaystyle=2\partial^{x}_{(\kappa}\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}\partial^{x}_{\lambda)}\varphi^{I}_{x}+2\partial^{x}_{(\kappa}\psi^{IA}_{xy}\partial_{\lambda)}^{x}\psi^{IB}_{xz}+m_{I}^{2}\eta_{\kappa\lambda}(\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}\varphi^{I}_{x}+\psi^{IA}_{xy}\psi^{IB}_{xz})\,,

(269)

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Theta^{N,I}_{2}\big]

\displaystyle=\psi^{NAB}_{xyz}(\varphi^{I}_{x})^{2}+2\psi^{NA}_{xy}\psi^{IB}_{xz}\varphi_{x}^{I}+2\psi_{xz}^{NB}\psi^{IA}_{xy}\varphi_{x}^{I}+2h_{x}^{N}\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}\varphi_{x}^{I}+2h_{x}^{N}\psi^{IA}_{xy}\psi^{IB}_{xz}\,,

\displaystyle\Delta^{A,B}_{y,z}\big[\Theta^{N,P,I}_{3}\big]

\displaystyle=2h^{(N}_{x}\psi^{P)AB}_{xyz}(\varphi_{x}^{I})^{2}+2\psi^{(N|A|}_{xy}\psi^{P)B}_{xz}(\varphi_{x}^{I})^{2}+2\varphi_{x}^{N}\psi^{PA}_{xy}\psi^{IB}_{xz}\varphi_{x}^{I}+4\psi^{(N|A|}_{xy}\psi^{I)B}_{xz}h_{x}^{P}\varphi_{x}^{I}

\displaystyle+2\psi_{xz}^{PB}\psi^{IA}_{xy}h^{N}_{x}\varphi_{x}^{I}+2h_{x}^{N}h_{x}^{P}\psi^{IAB}_{xyz}\varphi_{x}^{I}+2h_{x}^{N}h_{x}^{P}\psi^{IA}_{xy}\psi^{IB}_{xz}.

Appendix C
Explicit Form of the Quantum Recursion Relations

Here we collect the
\Omega,\Lambda,\Theta
currents for the quantum recursion relation of scalar field (
75
) and graviton (
77
).
The
\Omega
currents are

\displaystyle\Omega^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}\big|^{I}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=k^{\kappa}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\Big\lceil J^{\kappa\lambda},k^{\lambda}\Phi^{I}\Big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\Big\lceil J^{\lambda\lambda},\Phi^{I}\Big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

(270)

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell}\bigg[\frac{k^{\kappa}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}}{2}\Big(k_{\hat{\mathcal{P}},\ell}^{\lambda}\Psi^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}\big|_{\ell|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{I,\kappa\lambda}+\ell^{\lambda}\Psi^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}\big|_{\ell|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{\kappa\lambda,I}\Big)+\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\hat{\Psi}^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}\big|^{I,\lambda\lambda}_{\ell|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle\Omega^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}\big|^{IMN}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\Big\lceil J^{M}{,}J^{N}{,}\Phi^{I}\Big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\int_{\ell}\Big[2\big\lceil J^{M}{,}\hat{\Psi}_{\ell}^{IN}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\big\lceil\hat{\Psi}_{\ell}^{MN}{,}\Phi^{I}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\hat{\Psi}^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}\big|_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{MNI}\,,

\displaystyle\Omega^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}\big|^{IMNP}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\Big\lceil J^{M}{,}J^{N}{,}J^{P}{,}\Phi^{I}\Big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell}\left[3\big\lceil J^{(M}{,}J^{N}{,}\hat{\Psi}^{P),I}_{\ell}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\frac{3}{2}\Big\lceil\Delta^{(P}_{\ell}\big\lceil J^{M}{,}J^{N)}\big\rfloor{,}\Phi^{I}\Big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\right]

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\left[\big\lceil\hat{\Psi}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}^{MNP},\Phi^{I}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+3\big\lceil\hat{\Psi}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}^{I(MN},J^{P)}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+3\big\lceil\Psi^{(MN}_{\ell_{2}},\hat{\Psi}_{\ell_{1}}^{P)I}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\right]\,.

The
\Lambda
currents are

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{1,1}\big|^{\rho N,\sigma P}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\big\lceil k^{\rho}J^{N},k^{\sigma}J^{P}\big\rfloor^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\int_{\ell}k^{\rho}_{\ell}\ell^{\sigma}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}\big|_{\ell|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{N,P}\,,

(271)

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{1,2}\big|^{N,\rho\sigma,P}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\big\lceil J^{N},k^{\rho}k^{\sigma}J^{P}\big\rfloor^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\ell}\Big(k^{\rho}_{\ell}k^{\sigma}_{\ell}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}\big|^{P,N}_{\ell|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\ell^{\rho}\ell^{\sigma}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}\big|^{N,P}_{\ell|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\Big)\,,

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{2,1}\big|^{N,\rho P,\sigma Q}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\big\lceil J^{N},k^{\rho}J^{P},k^{\sigma}J^{Q}\big\rfloor^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\ell}\Delta^{N}_{\ell}\big\lceil k^{\rho}J^{P},k^{\sigma}J^{Q}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell}\ell^{\sigma}\Big[\big\lceil k^{\rho}J^{P},\Psi_{\ell}^{NQ}\big\rfloor^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\big\lceil J^{N},k^{\rho}_{\ell}\Psi_{\ell}^{PQ}\big\rfloor^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3}\int_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\bigg[k^{\rho}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}},\ell_{12}}\ell_{1}^{\sigma}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}\big|_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{PQN}+\ell_{2}^{\rho}k^{\sigma}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}},\ell_{12}}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}\big|_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{QNP}+\ell_{1}^{\rho}\ell_{2}^{\sigma}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}\big|^{NPQ}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{2,2}\big|^{N,P,\rho\sigma,Q}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\big\lceil J^{N},J^{P},k^{\rho}k^{\sigma}J^{Q}\big\rfloor^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell}\Big[\big\lceil k^{\rho}k^{\sigma}J^{Q},\Psi_{\ell}^{NP}\big\rfloor^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\big\lceil J^{N},k^{\rho}_{\ell}k^{\sigma}_{\ell}\Psi_{\ell}^{QP}+\ell^{\rho}\ell^{\sigma}\Psi_{\ell}^{PQ}\big\rfloor^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3}\int_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\bigg[\ell_{1}^{\rho}\ell_{1}^{\sigma}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}\big|_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{PQN}+k^{\rho}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}},\ell_{12}}k^{\sigma}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}},\ell_{12}}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}\big|_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{QNP}+\ell_{2}^{\rho}\ell_{2}^{\sigma}\Psi^{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}\big|_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{NPQ}\bigg]\,.

The
\Theta
currents are

\displaystyle\Theta^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{1}\big|^{\rho\sigma}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{I=1}^{2}\bigg[\big\lceil k^{\rho}\Phi^{I},k^{\sigma}\Phi^{I}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}-\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\eta^{\rho\sigma}\big[\Phi^{I},\Phi^{I}\big]^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\int_{\ell}\Big(k^{(\rho}_{\ell}\ell^{\sigma)}-\frac{m_{I}^{2}}{2}\eta^{\rho\sigma}\Big)\Psi^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}\big|_{\ell|\hat{\mathcal{P}}}^{II}\bigg]\,,

(272)

\displaystyle\Theta^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{2}\big|^{N}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{I=1}^{2}m_{I}^{2}\Bigg[\big\lceil J^{N},\Phi^{I},\Phi^{I}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\int_{\ell}\left(\big\lceil J^{N}{,}\Psi^{II}_{\ell}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+2\big\lceil\Phi^{I}{,}\hat{\Psi}^{IN}_{\ell}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\right)

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\hat{\Psi}^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\big|^{NII}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\Bigg]\,,

\displaystyle\Theta^{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}_{3}\big|^{N,P}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{I=1}^{2}m_{I}^{2}\Bigg[\big\lceil\Phi^{I}{,}\Phi^{I}{,}J^{N}{,}J^{P}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\int_{\ell}\Big(\big\lceil\Phi^{I}{,}\Phi^{I}{,}\Psi_{\ell}^{NP}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\big\lceil J^{N}{,}J^{P}{,}\Psi^{II}_{\ell}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell}\left(\Big\lceil\Delta^{I}_{\ell}\big\lceil J^{N}{,}J^{P}\big\rfloor,\Phi^{I}\Big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+\Big\lceil\Delta^{P}_{\ell}\big[\Phi^{I},\Phi^{I}\big],J^{N}\Big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-1)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\right)

\displaystyle+\int_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\bigg[\big\lceil\Psi_{\ell_{1}}^{NP},\Psi_{\ell_{2}}^{II}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+2\big\lceil\hat{\Psi}_{\ell_{1}}^{PI},\hat{\Psi}_{\ell_{2}}^{NI}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}

\displaystyle+2\big\lceil\Phi^{I},\hat{\Psi}^{NPI}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}+2\big\lceil J^{N},\hat{\Psi}^{PII}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}}\big\rfloor^{{\scriptscriptstyle(n-2)}}_{\hat{\mathcal{P}}}\bigg]\Bigg]\,.

Appendix D
IBP reduction of the Two loop integrals

In Section
6
, the classical amplitudes in each sector are represented by linear combinations of the master integrals through IBP reduction. Here, we denote the explicit forms of the IBP reduction of the two-loop integrands.

D.1
\Box\!\Box
class

The IBP reduction for each
I^{\rm class}_{i}

D.1.1
III sector

The coefficients are

\displaystyle c^{\text{III}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{m_{1}^{2}m_{2}^{2}\left(2\sigma^{2}-1\right)}{3\left(\sigma^{2}-1\right)^{2}\epsilon^{3}(2\epsilon-1)}\Big[c^{\rm III}_{1,1}(m_{1}^{2}+m_{2}^{2})+c^{\rm III}_{1,2}m_{1}m_{2}\Big]\,,

(273)

\displaystyle c^{\text{III}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{8m_{1}^{2}m_{2}^{2}}{\left(\sigma^{2}-1\right)^{3/2}\epsilon^{4}(2\epsilon-1)}\Big[c^{\rm III}_{2,1}(m_{1}^{2}+m_{2}^{2})+c^{\rm III}_{2,2}m_{1}m_{2}.\Big]\,,

\displaystyle c^{\text{III}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{m_{1}^{2}m_{2}^{2}\left(2\sigma^{2}-1\right)^{3}\left(\sigma\left(m_{1}^{2}+m_{2}^{2}\right)+2m_{1}m_{2}\right)}{\left(\sigma^{2}-1\right)^{3/2}\epsilon^{3}}

\displaystyle c^{\text{III}}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{2m_{1}^{2}m_{2}^{2}\left(2\sigma^{2}-1\right)}{3\left(\sigma.^{2}-1\right)^{2}\epsilon^{3}(2\epsilon-1)}\Big[c^{\rm III}_{4,1}(m_{1}^{2}+m_{2}^{2})+c^{\rm III}_{4,2}m_{1}m_{2}\Big]\,,

\displaystyle c^{\text{III}}_{5}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2m_{1}^{2}m_{2}^{2}\left(2\sigma^{2}-1\right)}{3\left(\sigma^{2}-1\right)^{3/2}\epsilon^{3}(2\epsilon-1)}\Big[c^{\rm III}_{5,1}(m_{1}^{2}+m_{2}^{2})+c^{\rm III}_{5,2}m_{1}m_{2}\Big]\,,

\displaystyle c^{\text{III}}_{6}

Appendix E
Master Integrals

In this section, we present the explicit form of the master integrals at two loops derived in
Parra-Martinez:2020dzs
by solving the differential equation with the static boundary conditions in the potential region. Here, we modified the results in our convention. We have shown in Section
6.3
that the master integrals in the
IY
and

IY

classes can be represented by the master integrals in the
\Box\!\Box
and
\mathbf{H}
classes. Thus, we omit the master integrals for the
IY
and

IY

classes here.

We first consider the
\Box\!\Box
class, which consists of six sectors: III,
\overline{\rm III}
, IX,
\overline{\rm IX}
, XI and
\overline{\rm XI}
sectors. The XI and
\overline{\rm XI}
are the horizontal mirror dual of IX,
\overline{\rm IX}
, and we also omit them. The non-vanishing master integrals for the III sector are given by

\displaystyle f^{\text{III}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{\epsilon}{3}\log x-\epsilon^{2}\left(\text{Li}_{2}\left(1-x^{2}\right)+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

(291)

\displaystyle f^{\text{III}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{2\epsilon}{3}\log x+\frac{2\epsilon^{2}}{3}\left(\text{Li}_{2}\left(1-x^{2}\right)+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f^{\text{III}}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{3}+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{18}\left(48\log^{2}x+7\pi^{2}\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f^{\text{III}}_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{6}+\frac{7\pi^{2}\epsilon^{2}}{36}\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f^{\text{III},}_{7}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{12}\left(\pi^{2}-4\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f^{\text{III}}_{10}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{i\epsilon\pi}{4}-\frac{i\pi\epsilon^{2}}{2}\log 2\bigg]\,,

The non-vanishing master integrals for the
\overline{\rm III}
sector are given by

\displaystyle f^{\overline{\text{III}}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{\epsilon}{6}\log x+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}\left(\text{Li}_{2}\left(1-x^{2}\right)+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

(292)

\displaystyle f^{\overline{\text{III}}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{\epsilon}{3}\log x-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{3}\left(\text{Li}_{2}\left(1-x^{2}\right)+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f^{\overline{\text{III}}}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{6}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{36}\left(7\pi^{2}+48\pi^{2}\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f^{\overline{\text{III}}}_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{6}+\frac{7\pi^{2}\epsilon^{2}}{36}\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f^{\overline{\text{III}}}_{7}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{6}\log^{2}x\bigg]\,,

The non-vanishing master integrals for the IX sector are given by

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{6}\epsilon\log x-\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

(293)

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{3}\epsilon\log x+\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{6}+\frac{1}{36}\epsilon^{2}\left(7\pi^{2}+48\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},5}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{3}\epsilon\log x-\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{2}{3}\epsilon\log x+\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},7}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{18}\epsilon^{2}\left(7\pi^{2}+48\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},8}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{6}+\frac{7\pi^{2}\epsilon^{2}}{36}\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},10}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{5}{12}\epsilon^{2}\log^{2}(x)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\mathrm{IX},15}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{i\pi\epsilon}{4}-\frac{i\pi\log(2)\epsilon^{2}}{2}\bigg]\,.

The non-vanishing master integrals for the
\overline{\rm IX}
sector are given by

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\mathrm{IX}},2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{3}\epsilon\log x+\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

(294)

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\mathrm{IX}},3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{2}{3}\epsilon\log x-\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\mathrm{IX}},4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{18}\epsilon^{2}\left(7\pi^{2}+48\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\mathrm{IX}},5}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{6}\epsilon\log x+\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\mathrm{IX}},6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{3}\epsilon\log x-\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\mathrm{IX}},7}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{6}+\frac{1}{36}\epsilon^{2}\left(7\pi^{2}+48\log^{2}(x)\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\mathrm{IX}},8}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{6}+\frac{7\pi^{2}\epsilon^{2}}{36}\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\mathrm{IX}},10}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{2}\log^{2}(x)\bigg]\,.

Next, we consider the
\mathbf{H}
class. As discussed in Section
6.2
, the master integrals in
\mathbf{H}
class are decomposed into the even and odd powers in
|q|
. Since the odd terms do not contribute to the total amplitude, we only focus on the even terms.

The non-vanishing master integrals for the H sector are given by

\displaystyle f_{\rm H,4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{4}\bigg]\,,

(295)

\displaystyle f_{\rm H,5}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{3}\epsilon\log x-\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\rm H,6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{2}{3}\epsilon\log x+\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\rm H,7}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{3}+\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{7\pi^{2}}{18}+\frac{8}{3}\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\rm H,9}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{2}{3}\epsilon\log x+\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\rm H,10}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\left(\pi^{2}+8\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,.

Similarly, the non-vanishing master integrals for the
\overline{\rm H}
sector are given by

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\rm H},4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{4}\bigg]\,,

(296)

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\rm H},5}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{6}\epsilon\log x+\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\rm H},6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\frac{1}{3}\epsilon\log x+\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\rm H},7}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{6}-\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{7\pi^{2}}{36}+\frac{4}{3}\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\rm H},9}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[\frac{1}{3}\epsilon\log x-\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{2}\left(\operatorname{Li}_{2}(1-x^{2})+\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,,

\displaystyle f_{\overline{\rm H},10}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon^{2}\pi^{2}}{(q^{2})^{2\epsilon}}\bigg[-\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\pi^{2}+2\log^{2}x\right)\bigg]\,.
